        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-9-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: artagents/orchestrate/author_test.py\n+\"\"\"Author-test fixture resolution and compiled-plan loading.\n+\n+The runtime runner lands in later Phase 9 batches. This module keeps the\n+filesystem decisions isolated: fixture/golden layout preference and loading a\n+compiled plan without ever generating pack-local build artifacts on demand.\n+\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from dataclasses import dataclass\n+from pathlib import Path\n+from typing import Literal\n+\n+from artagents.core.task.plan import TaskPlan, TaskPlanError, load_plan\n+\n+from .compile import DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT, _qualified_split, compile_to_path\n+from .dsl import OrchestrateDefinitionError\n+\n+\n+FixtureLayout = Literal[\"orchestrator\", \"pack\"]\n+PlanSource = Literal[\"committed_build\", \"scratch_compile\"]\n+\n+\n+class AuthorTestError(RuntimeError):\n+    \"\"\"Raised when an author-test target cannot be resolved or loaded.\"\"\"\n+\n+\n+@dataclass(frozen=True)\n+class AuthorTestPaths:\n+    pack_id: str\n+    orchestrator_id: str\n+    fixture_name: str\n+    qualified_id: str\n+    packs_root: Path\n+    pack_root: Path\n+    fixture_dir: Path\n+    golden_path: Path\n+    fixture_layout: FixtureLayout\n+    committed_plan_path: Path\n+    source_path: Path\n+\n+\n+@dataclass(frozen=True)\n+class LoadedAuthorTestPlan:\n+    plan: TaskPlan\n+    plan_path: Path\n+    source: PlanSource\n+\n+\n+@dataclass(frozen=True)\n+class AuthorTestTarget:\n+    paths: AuthorTestPaths\n+    loaded_plan: LoadedAuthorTestPlan\n+\n+\n+def resolve_author_test_paths(\n+    pack_id: str,\n+    orchestrator_id: str,\n+    fixture_name: str,\n+    *,\n+    packs_root: Path | None = None,\n+) -> AuthorTestPaths:\n+    \"\"\"Resolve fixture, golden, source, and committed-build paths.\n+\n+    Orchestrator-scoped fixtures are preferred:\n+    ``<pack>/<orchestrator>/fixtures/<fixture>/`` and matching golden under\n+    ``<pack>/<orchestrator>/golden/``. If that fixture directory is absent, the\n+    legacy pack-root layout is used instead.\n+    \"\"\"\n+\n+    fixture_name = _validate_path_segment(fixture_name, \"fixture\")\n+    qualified_id = _qualified_id(pack_id, orchestrator_id)\n+    pack_id, orchestrator_id = _qualified_split(qualified_id)\n+    root = Path(packs_root) if packs_root is not None else DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT\n+    pack_root = root / pack_id\n+    if not pack_root.is_dir():\n+        raise AuthorTestError(\n+            f\"author test {qualified_id}: pack directory not found at {pack_root}\"\n+        )\n+\n+    scoped_fixture = pack_root / orchestrator_id / \"fixtures\" / fixture_name\n+    scoped_golden = (\n+        pack_root / orchestrator_id / \"golden\" / f\"{fixture_name}.events.jsonl\"\n+    )\n+    pack_fixture = pack_root / \"fixtures\" / fixture_name\n+    pack_golden = pack_root / \"golden\" / f\"{fixture_name}.events.jsonl\"\n+\n+    if scoped_fixture.is_dir():\n+        fixture_dir = scoped_fixture\n+        golden_path = scoped_golden\n+        fixture_layout: FixtureLayout = \"orchestrator\"\n+    elif pack_fixture.is_dir():\n+        fixture_dir = pack_fixture\n+        golden_path = pack_golden\n+        fixture_layout = \"pack\"\n+    else:\n+        raise AuthorTestError(\n+            f\"author test {qualified_id} --fixture {fixture_name}: fixture \"\n+            f\"directory not found; checked {scoped_fixture} and {pack_fixture}\"\n+        )\n+\n+    return AuthorTestPaths(\n+        pack_id=pack_id,\n+        orchestrator_id=orchestrator_id,\n+        fixture_name=fixture_name,\n+        qualified_id=qualified_id,\n+        packs_root=root,\n+        pack_root=pack_root,\n+        fixture_dir=fixture_dir,\n+        golden_path=golden_path,\n+        fixture_layout=fixture_layout,\n+        committed_plan_path=pack_root / \"build\" / f\"{orchestrator_id}.json\",\n+        source_path=pack_root / f\"{orchestrator_id}.py\",\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def load_author_test_plan(\n+    paths: AuthorTestPaths,\n+    *,\n+    scratch_dir: Path | None = None,\n+) -> LoadedAuthorTestPlan:\n+    \"\"\"Load the committed build, or compile source into scratch and load it.\"\"\"\n+\n+    if paths.committed_plan_path.is_file():\n+        return LoadedAuthorTestPlan(\n+            plan=_load_plan(paths.committed_plan_path, paths.qualified_id),\n+            plan_path=paths.committed_plan_path,\n+            source=\"committed_build\",\n+        )\n+\n+    if not paths.source_path.is_file():\n+        raise AuthorTestError(\n+            f\"author test {paths.qualified_id}: compiled plan not found at \"\n+            f\"{paths.committed_plan_path}, and source module not found at \"\n+            f\"{paths.source_path}\"\n+        )\n+    if scratch_dir is None:\n+        raise AuthorTestError(\n+            f\"author test {paths.qualified_id}: scratch_dir is required when \"\n+            \"the committed build artifact is missing\"\n+        )\n+\n+    scratch_build_path = (\n+        Path(scratch_dir) / \"build\" / paths.pack_id / f\"{paths.orchestrator_id}.json\"\n+    )\n+    try:\n+        compiled_path = compile_to_path(\n+ paths.qualified_id),\n+        plan_path=compiled_path,\n+        source=\"scratch_compile\",\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def load_author_test_target(\n+    pack_id: str,\n+    orchestrator_id: str,\n+    fixture_name: str,\n+    *,\n+    packs_root: Path | None = None,\n+    scratch_dir: Path | None = None,\n+) -> AuthorTestTarget:\n+    \"\"\"Resolve paths and load the author-test plan in one call.\"\"\"\n+\n+    paths = resolve_author_test_paths(\n+        pack_id,\n+        orchestrator_id,\n+        fixture_name,\n+        packs_root=packs_root,\n+    )\n+    return AuthorTestTarget(\n+        paths=paths,\n+        loaded_plan=load_author_test_plan(paths, scratch_dir=scratch_dir),\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def _load_plan(path: Path, qualified_id: str) -> TaskPlan:\n+    try:\n+        return load_plan(path)\n+    except TaskPlanError as exc:\n+        raise AuthorTestError(\n+            f\"author test {qualified_id}: invalid compiled plan at {path}: {exc}\"\n+        ) from exc\n+\n+\n+def _qualified_id(pack_id: str, orchestrator_id: str) -> str:\n+    try:\n+        pack, name = _qualified_split(f\"{pack_id}.{orchestrator_id}\")\n+    except OrchestrateDefinitionError as exc:\n+        raise AuthorTestError(f\"author test: {exc}\") from exc\n+    return f\"{pack}.{name}\"\n+\n+\n+def _validate_path_segment(value: str, label: str) -> str:\n+    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:\n+        raise AuthorTestError(f\"author test: {label} name must be non-empty\")\n+    path = Path(value)\n+    if path.is_absolute() or len(path.parts) != 1 or value in {\".\", \"..\"}:\n+        raise AuthorTestError(\n+            f\"author test: {label} name {value!r} must be a single path segment\"\n+        )\n+    return value\n*** End Patch\n"

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-9-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-9-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

_child_env(scratch: AuthorTestScratch) -> dict[str, str]:\n+    env = child_subprocess_env()\n+    env.update(_author_test_env_overrides(scratch))\n+    existing = env.get(\"PYTHONPATH\")\n+    if existing:\n+        env[\"PYTHONPATH\"] = os.pathsep.join([str(REPO_ROOT), existing])\n+    else:\n+        env[\"PYTHONPATH\"] = str(REPO_ROOT)\n+    return env\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

replacements: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]) -> Any:\n+    if isinstance(value, dict):\n+        return {\n+            [REDACTED](item, replacements)\n+            for key, item in value.items()\n+ return _replace_paths(value, replacements)\n+    return value\n+\n+\n+def _path_replacements(\n+    *,\n+    run_dir: str | Path | None,\n+    fixture_dir: str | Path | None,\n+) -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:\n+    pairs: list[tuple[str, str]] = []\n+    if run_dir is not None:\n+        pairs.extend(_single_path_replacements(Path(run_dir), RUN_DIR_TOKEN))\n+    if fixture_dir is not None:\n+        pairs.extend(_single_path_replacements(Path(fixture_dir), FIXTURE_DIR_TOKEN))\n+    return tuple(sorted(set(pairs), key=lambda item: len(item[0]), reverse=True))\n+\n+\n+def _single_path_replacements(path: Path, token: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:\n+    candidates = {str(path)}\n+    try:\n+        candidates.add(str(path.resolve()))\n+    except OSError:\n+        pass\n+    return [(candidate.rstrip(os.sep), token) for candidate in candidates if candidate]\n+\n+\n+def _replace_paths(value: str, replacements: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]) -> str:\n+    out = value\n+    for prefix, token in replacements:\n+        out = out.replace(prefix + os.sep, token + \"/\")\n+        out = out.replace(prefix, token)\n+    return out\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-9-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-9-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

line:\\n{out}\")\n+        print(\"drift repro ok\")\n+    return 0\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    raise SystemExit(main())\n*** End Patch\n"
